# The public API of the mod in the folder 'core': every type and member a mod
# built on it can reach. The number before a line is the version of the API that brought
# it in the shape written there. Whatever came after the stable version carries
# @ApiStatus.Experimental; the stable version rises only once what came up to
# it meets the bar docs/API.md sets for a stable part. Kept by the API surface test,
# which writes to build/api-photo what this file would have to say when the API changes.
stable 4
